| COPY-READY GPT / CURSOR PROMPT |
| TASK: Rovvy Flights — Complete Sprint 1: Redirect-only product alignment

AUTHORITATIVE PROJECT: D:\group travel os
READ FIRST: AGENTS.md, .cursorrules, GEMINI.md, CLAUDE.md, Scram Book\README.md, Scram Book\Travel Tab\Rovvy_Travel_Flights_Scrum_Plan.xlsx (Sprint 1), and Rovvy_Flights_Detailed_Product_Report.docx.

OBJECTIVE
Make the only public Flights journey: search → compare itineraries → view seller options → open the chosen authorized external provider. Rovvy must not collect traveler/payment data, create a flight order, or require sign-in merely to leave for a provider. Preserve existing brand and search/results UI unless a change is required for this boundary.

REQUIRED CHANGES
1. In provider normalization and the results fallback, emit external_redirect only for a validated HTTPS redirect URL. When no redirect exists (including Duffel sandbox), emit unavailable—not provider_checkout.
2. In FlightOptionsDrawer.tsx remove getToken/authHref/login restoration, providerCheckoutHref, internal /flights/offer navigation, and all provider_checkout CTAs. Show an external link only when actionable; otherwise show a disabled Test offer / external link unavailable state.
3. Add a centralized, disabled internal-checkout product-mode guard. Direct public visits to /flights/offer/*, /flights/checkout/* and /flights/booking/* must redirect to /flights or render not-found while Sprint 1 is active. Do not edit any .env file. Historical booking code may remain unreachable for reference.
4. Standardize public copy: View options, Compare providers, Continue to provider. Beside every enabled redirect state: “You’ll complete payment and manage tickets, changes, cancellations, and support with [seller]. Rovvy does not issue or service tickets.” Never show Complete your booking, Pay & Book, Secure booking through Rovvy, or similar claims on a reachable public Flights screen.
5. Replace tests that currently expect provider_checkout, /flights/offer login restoration, or sign-in-to-continue. Add regression coverage for: guest comparison/redirect, missing redirect disabled, sandbox disclosure, expired offer disabled, internal route guard, prohibited copy, and external HTTPS handoff.
6. Do not invent Priceline, Expedia, MakeMyTrip, EaseMyTrip, Agoda, airline-direct, or other sellers. A provider appears only with authorized configured data and a real itinerary-specific redirect. Do not scrape providers.

FILES TO INSPECT (not an automatic permission to rewrite unrelated code)
frontend/components/travel/FlightOptionsDrawer.tsx; frontend/app/(dashboard)/flights/results/page.tsx; frontend/app/(dashboard)/flights/offer/[offerId]/; frontend/app/(dashboard)/flights/checkout/; frontend/app/(dashboard)/flights/booking/; frontend/lib/__tests__/flight-metasearch.test.ts; frontend/lib/__tests__/flight-amadeus-ui.test.ts; app/services/flight_providers/normalizer.py; tests/test_flight_metasearch.py.

VERIFICATION
Run the focused Flights frontend tests, relevant backend flight-metasearch tests, and npx tsc --noEmit. Report exact commands and results. Search reachable public Flights files for prohibited booking language and internal checkout links. Update GEMINI.md/.cursorrules registries only with verified totals. Update Sprint 1 in the Scram Book with actual evidence, completion percentage, carry-over, and review decision.

DEFINITION OF DONE
No public result or provider option can enter Rovvy checkout; no guest is asked to sign in to redirect; test offers without links are disabled and honest; provider responsibility is explicit; direct internal checkout URLs are blocked; regression tests prevent reversal. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

| SPRINT 2 STATUS — FL-074 is complete. FL-045 is in progress: the official Travelpayouts white-label embed is implemented and lint-verified, while production-domain rendering and live handoff verification remain pending. All other provider-governance controls remain planned and must not be marked complete without verified implementation and tests. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| CONNECTED REGISTER — 'Sprint 2 Provider Pipeline' now contains 1,250 actual named candidates: 12 distribution/booking/search platforms and 1,238 unique airline/operator names. It separately records Rovvy code presence, credential verification, official API evidence and current usability. Provider names do not imply direct connection or authorization. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

| Matching offers are grouped accurately without merging different journeys. |

| Scope: 12 booking/distribution/search platforms plus 1,238 unique named airline/operator candidates. A named provider is not automatically connected. API, credentials, authorization, display rights and live usability are evaluated independently. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

| Evidence rule: ‘API not verified’ is not the same as ‘No API.’ ‘Implemented’ means Rovvy code exists; it does not prove a valid key or live authorization. ‘Usable now’ requires verified credentials, permission and a successful permitted environment check. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Source limitation: OpenFlights provides broad worldwide airline/operator names but states that its active flag is unreliable and its public snapshot is sporadically updated. The official IATA page currently lists 378 members; ICAO identifies its paid/API data service as the official operator-code source. Therefore airline rows remain API-unverified until separately researched. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

## Sheet: Sprint 2 Wave 1 Audit
| Sprint 2 — Wave 1 Provider Verification |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Verified 2026-08-27 · Search-only audit · No traveler, payment, order, or booking calls |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Provider | Provider type | Code path | Credential | Live API | Offers | External redirect | Wave 1 decision | Evidence | Authorization / blocker | Next action | Official source |
| Duffel | Flight inventory / order API | Implemented | Configured | Passed (sandbox) | 1 | No | Search verified; not usable as redirect-only seller | ORD–RJA–ORD, 2026-10-15 to 2026-11-15; 1 priced offer; 25 focused tests passed. | Returned a priced test offer, but Duffel offers are intended for order creation and contain no OTA seller handoff URL. | Keep non-actionable in redirect-only mode; do not expose Continue until a separately authorized external link exists. | https://duffel.com/docs/api/v2/offer-requests |
| Kiwi.com Tequila | Third-party flight search / deep-link API | Implemented | Configured key rejected | Failed — HTTP 401 | 0 | No | Blocked | ORD–RJA–ORD, 2026-10-15 to 2026-11-15; live request rejected. | Configured key is unauthorized or no longer valid. Kiwi states new Tequila partnerships are invitation-only. | Contact Kiwi partnerships; obtain active authorization/key; retest before enabling. | https://media.kiwi.com/articles-and-interviews/better-for-business-kiwi-com-takes-a-new-approach-to-partnerships/ |
| Travelpayouts / Aviasales | Affiliate flight-price data + external links | Implemented | Configured; authenticated | HTTP 200; success=true; 0 cached offers | 0 | No — no offers returned | Authentication verified; inventory coverage pending | 2026-08-28: token present; ORD–RJA and ORD–LON returned valid successful responses with zero cached fares; airport autocomplete returned 5 Chicago suggestions. | The Data API is working, but no itinerary row or redirect can be validated until cached inventory is returned. | Keep non-actionable; test additional popular routes/date windows and validate tp.media HTTPS handoff only when an offer is returned. | https://support.travelpayouts.com/hc/en-us/categories/200358578-API-and-data |
| Wave 1 conclusion | 0 of 3 providers is currently eligible as a live selectable redirect seller; Travelpayouts authentication is now verified. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Reason | Duffel has no external redirect; Kiwi is not configured; Travelpayouts authenticates but returned zero cached fare rows. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Release gate | Authorization + successful live response + itinerary-specific HTTPS link + domain allowlist + failure-state tests. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
## Sheet: Sprint 2 Recovery
| Sprint 2 — Zero-result Route Recovery |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Verified 2026-08-30 · Exact provider search remains authoritative; alternatives are transparent new searches, never fabricated offers. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| ID | Goal / Control | Implementation | Verified Result | Status | Evidence | Risk / Constraint | Next Action |
| FL-075 | Zero-result recovery | Exact search → airports within 500 km → national gateways → international gateway | HLC→KHG returned 7 bounded alternative searches | Complete | Backend response + browser DOM | Alternatives are not confirmed itineraries | Measure which recovered searches return provider fares |
| FL-076 | No fabricated prices | Recovery objects contain route/date/reason only; no price field | UI states price appears only after an authorized provider returns it | Complete | FlightRouteRecoveryOption schema + UI disclosure | Travelpayouts cache may have no fare | Keep empty state transparent |
| FL-077 | Airport substitution safety | Travelpayouts rows that change requested origin/destination are excluded from exact results | Nearby/city substitutions no longer masquerade as exact offers | Complete | Travelpayouts provider adapter | May reduce exact result count | Present substitutions only through recovery |
| FL-078 | Currency integrity | Use response/row currency instead of relabeling requested currency | Parser now retains provider-reported currency | Complete | Travelpayouts parser | No FX conversion is performed | Add exchange-rate service only after authorization |
| QA-202 | Recovery regression tests | Nearby origin/destination, bounded result count, gateway warnings, no round-trip invention | 33 focused backend tests passed; TypeScript passed | Passed | pytest + npx tsc --noEmit --incremental false | Pytest cache warnings only | Add component click test in later hardening |
| UI-202 | Recovery presentation | Responsive cards with route, distance, explanation and separate-ticket warning | Browser rendered 7 cards and navigated HLC→KHG to HYS→KHG | Passed | Local browser verification | Click starts another provider search; it does not guarantee inventory | Add flexible-date cards in next increment |
| Product decision: Continue Sprint 2. This feature improves zero-result recovery but does not replace provider authorization or create live inventory. Duffel remains disabled in the current runtime; Travelpayouts is the only enabled provider. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
